## 2026-08-15 Bounded next steps and deferred security debt

本阶段完整离线验证已完成：`uv run pytest tests -q` → `2762 passed, 1 skipped` in `149.42s`；full JavaScript 为 `128 passed, 0 failed`。一次被忽略但必需的 Python.org screenshot fixture 已从 `logs` 下已有的 SHA-256 完全相同副本恢复；这是测试证据恢复，不是代码变更或提交产物。focused 证据为下位分组：form `286 passed`、learning `412 passed`、navigation/SEEK offline `443 passed`、JavaScript `104 passed`。

1. 先保持当前公开范围为 Quick Apply `open_apply_flow` 入口、审核语义 dry-run 和 no-submit safe-stop；完整离线回归虽已完成，仍不要把它写成真实 SEEK、live Demo 或 unattended reliability。
2. 修复受管 artifacts 的本地写权限边界：建立内容寻址且不可静默替换的受管证据存储，明确允许的 materialization/path replacement、owner、源字节 SHA-256 和 provenance 绑定；路径、内容或 provenance 无法证明时必须继续 `needs_human_review` / invalid case。完成后补跨路径、同路径改内容、overlay/materialization 和重放回归，再考虑扩大公开行为。
3. 修复单 API owner 模型下 registry 的并发 stale save：在 registry 更新、后台 rebuild 和 review save 之间加入服务端 revision/owner epoch 的 compare-and-swap（或等价单写入序列），拒绝旧版本覆盖新版本。未完成前继续禁止多 worker/多进程共享 owner；版本不明确时 fail closed，不复活批准。
4. 两项债务关闭后，重新运行四组 focused/offline 检查并分别保留 negative controls；另行审批后才可考虑当前窗口的 live no-submit rehearsal。Gate、Operation、Execute 和 final-submit/no-submit 边界不得因债务修复而放宽。

详细风险说明与完成条件见 `docs/DEFERRED_SECURITY_DEBT.md`。

## 2026-07-09 Stage1 adjacent partition rule

User-added invariant: Stage1 structure bars must form adjacent page partitions before Stage2 item numbering. Empty visible space belongs to a structure region; Stage1 must not shrink `main_content` to only the visible card/text cluster. Content boxes can be children of a bar, but cannot redefine the bar bbox.

Implemented in `app\learn\recognition\stage1_audit.py`:

- `main_content` must be adjacent to the left boundary / left sidebar.
- `main_content` must start at the top-bar bottom boundary.
- `main_content` must cover the right empty visible area until the right boundary / right sidebar.
- `main_content` must cover the lower empty visible area until the bottom boundary / bottom bar.
- Failure categories include `main_content_not_adjacent_to_left_boundary`, `main_content_not_adjacent_to_top_boundary`, `main_content_does_not_cover_right_empty_area`, and `main_content_does_not_cover_lower_empty_area`.

Verification:

- `uv run pytest tests\test_learn_stage1_region_selection_audit.py tests\test_web_panel_route.py::test_learning_interface_flow_has_unified_progress_and_simple_review_surface tests\test_learn_page_detail_candidate.py::test_page_detail_candidate_preserves_panel_trial_overlay_and_screenshot -q`: 6 passed.
- `uv run python -m py_compile app\learn\recognition\stage1_audit.py app\api\panel.py scripts\build_learn_page_detail_candidate.py`: passed.
- `node --check app\web_panel\panel.js`: passed.

AppleMusic `223051` Stage1 gate replay with the new rule produces adjacent regions: left nav `x=0,w=92`, top bar `x=92,y=0,w=1062,h=90`, primary area `x=92,y=90,w=1062,h=911`. Overlay: `artifacts\review-overlays\apple-music__observe-screen__applemusic__full-window__20260709-223051-047459__two-stage-understanding__20260709-224339-991446.png`.

Runtime note: the real panel did not show boxes because the running uvicorn process was still serving old code. The panel server was restarted on port `8000`; replaying the real `223131` request now saves `coordinate_overlay_path` into `trial_result.json` and `learn_page_detail_candidate.json`. Evidence: `artifacts\learning-runs\panel_20260709-224454-531_applemusic\trial_result.json` and `artifacts\learning-runs\panel_20260709-224454-531_applemusic\learn_page_detail_candidate.json`.

ChatGPT send calibration note: in the current Codex split layout, `composer_point=(1105,1117)` is a safe GPT text-box point, not the send button. The calibrated framework send point is `send_point=(1310,1108)`, but clicking it is not considered proof of delivery. The calibration screenshot is `artifacts\review-overlays\chatgpt_send_calibration_codex_window_20260708.png`; use `scripts\send_chatgpt_visual_audit.py --flow-preset codex_split_20260708` for the fixed reviewer flow and verify latest user message prompt token plus image count after sending.

2026-07-08 reviewer flow is scripted for fixed-point paste and guarded against false-positive sends. Use `scripts\send_chatgpt_visual_audit.py` for the ChatGPT visual-audit path instead of manually mixing bridge focus, file input, and clipboard image paste. The script defaults to `--dry-run`; `--flow-preset codex_split_20260708 --send --no-dry-run` runs the calibrated order: framework click GPT composer, paste text with `click_before_typing=false` after that explicit click, framework click GPT composer before each `paste_image(..., focus_bound_window=false)`, then click the calibrated send point. The preset forces image refocus and reports `focus_contract.strict_composer_focus=true`, `prompt_hidden_click_before_typing=false`, and `fixed_flow_refocus_before_each_image_forced=true`. The JSON result deliberately reports `send_click_attempted=true` and `sent=false` until a separate ChatGPT DOM/visible-thread check confirms the latest user message prompt token and expected image count. The script now also emits `planned_action_sequence` in dry-run and `actual_action_sequence` in real runs; check these fields before treating a reviewer send as valid. The v98 dry-run evidence for three page-detail preview images is `logs\benchmarks\stage2_v98_fixed_flow_dry_run.json`.

2026-07-07 parent/child boundary contract is now stricter: Stage1 bars/regions own their Stage2 internal content, partial overflow is clipped and review-marked, and fully out-of-parent content is rejected instead of being displayed as a fake 1px clipped box. `child_scope=inside_parent|clipped_to_parent|outside_parent_rejected` is now the machine-readable relationship. This keeps the rule generic: child boxes may overlap only when a real parent contains them; sibling bars/groups must not explain overlap as normal. Next recognition work should preserve this invariant while improving Stage2 internal web/card/list hierarchy; do not loosen it to make overlays look fuller.

2026-07-06 Stage1-only region localization checkpoint completed. A new runner, `scripts\run_learn_stage1_region_localization.py`, can replay an existing learn-mode observe trace and stop after top-level region localization. It deliberately skips Stage 2 numbering and PathGraph draft generation, so offset debugging is isolated to column/structure-region boxes. Latest Apple Music replay produced `screen_inventory_count=68`, `localized_region_count=3`, and `geometry_only_region_count=0`; report: `logs\learn_stage1_region_localization\stage1_region_localization_report_20260706-211439.json`; overlay: `artifacts\review-overlays\apple-music__observe-screen__applemusic__full-window__20260706-200734-125573__stage1-region-localization__20260706-211439-344665.png`.

The current stage1 calibration now treats coarse `screen_map.sections` as hints, adds OCR text evidence, separates left navigation from main content, clamps top/header before content title, and merges `role=content` bottom sections back into primary content when they are visually continuous scroll content. This improves the visible Apple Music y-offset issue, but it is still heuristic review evidence. It is not a model/VISTA grounding accuracy result, not Execute authorization, and not a Runtime PathGraph promotion.

One actual Qwen3-VL 8B stage1 model probe has now run on the same fixed screenshot through `scripts\run_learn_stage1_region_model_probe.py`. After removing `heuristic_precise_bbox` from the prompt input to avoid answer leakage, the model returned 3 regions and treated `专属精选推荐`, `最近播放`, and `电子游戏音乐` as main-content evidence. Report: `logs\learn_stage1_region_model_probe\stage1_region_model_probe_report_20260706-211658.json`; overlay: `logs\learn_stage1_region_model_probe\apple-music__observe-screen__applemusic__full-window__20260706-200734-125573__stage1-region-model-probe__20260706-211658.png`.

2026-07-06 Stage2 numbering localization checkpoint now uses a scoped three-step policy: header/top bar and side navigation use direct whole-region numbering, while only the primary/main content region gets homogeneous grouping plus framework `visual_card_segmenter` media rows before model numbering. The earlier model-only grouping failure split cards into horizontal image/text bands, and subgrouping the top/side rails made simple controls noisier, so subgrouping is now restricted to the main content where mixed card sizes actually need it. A gated `visual_small_control_segmenter` now protects direct non-primary regions from sparse-toolbar model drift: the latest top bar had `visual_refinement.applied=true` with 13/13 refined controls, while left nav stayed unchanged with `reason=insufficient_visual_candidates`. Latest Apple Music probe report: `logs\learn_stage2_numbering_model_probe\stage2_numbering_model_probe_report_20260706-221519.json`; overlay: `logs\learn_stage2_numbering_model_probe\apple-music__observe-screen__applemusic__full-window__20260706-200734-125573__stage2-numbering-model-probe__20260706-221519.png`; result: `numbered_item_count=34`, `outside_parent_bbox_count=0`, `parse_error_count=0`. The same policy is now integrated into `app.learn.recognition.two_stage`, so formal learning drafts can expose `subregion_groups`, `visual_small_control_refinement`, and fused overlay evidence through page details. Visual self-check: top bar, left rail, and the first two media-card rows are usable display evidence; section-title boxes and partially visible lower rows still need refinement. Next useful work: improve section/title localization and bottom partial-row handling, then test the same formal Learning Draft path on a second app/webpage.

2026-07-06 Learning Mode two-stage understanding scaffold completed. The learning-recognition pipeline now emits `learn_two_stage_screen_understanding_v1`: Stage 1 coarse structure regions, Stage 1 whole-region localization for each structure area, Stage 2 per-region numbered items, and a fused review-only overlay exposed through `page_details.pipeline_audit.precise_understanding_fusion_status`. The Learning Draft screenshot preview refreshes the fusion overlay immediately after Learn Deep and restores it after the second draft-generation pass, so boxes no longer require clicking lower panel entries to appear.

The latest Apple Music observe trace was replayed offline through the new scaffold. The regenerated result produced 5 structure regions, 5 localized structure regions, 39 numbered items, 44 fused review boxes, and an overlay at `artifacts\review-overlays\apple-music__observe-screen__applemusic__full-window__20260706-200734-125573__two-stage-understanding__20260706-204339-299621.png`. A geometry correction now keeps left navigation icons in `left_nav` even if an upstream layout graph misassigns them to `main_content`, so the main-content structure box is not dragged to the far left.

Blocked visual candidates are no longer allowed into Learn Deep executable targets. For left-rail semantic icon guesses, the model-provided label is suppressed from review display and the screenshot scan supplies generic `nav_rail_icon_review_only` boxes instead. This keeps the UI honest: left icons can be inspected, but they are not treated as learned actionable targets.

Next useful work: replace the deterministic Stage 1 / Stage 1 localization / Stage 2 generators with real multi-call model prompts, starting with page-structure output, whole-region localization for each structure area, and then per-region numbering. Keep the same output contract and run the same overlay self-checks after each run. Continue to keep the result display-only until OCR/VISTA/rerank/Gate dry-run evidence validates specific regions.

2026-07-06 Learning Mode UI workflow scaffold checkpoint completed. The Learning Draft subview now presents the approved single flow as one button, `学习当前界面 / Learn current interface`, with progress states for bind/screenshot -> full-screen understanding -> numbered map -> page details -> precise calibration -> fusion -> read-only PathGraph draft -> complete. The existing split controls remain available, but are moved into a default-collapsed `Advanced diagnostics` disclosure so the demo surface is not dominated by debug tools.

The replay view has been simplified around the artifacts the user needs to inspect: history learning drafts, screenshot/numbered map, read-only PathGraph preview, interface detail, and a small manual edit form. The manual form saves an auditable `manual_edit` patch on the reviewed candidate; it does not turn the draft into an executable template by itself. Verified with static panel tests, JS syntax check, core panel route/static tests, and an in-app browser DOM smoke. No model run, live click, live fill, submit, Execute binding, or Runtime PathGraph promotion was performed.

Left navigation cleanup completed: Learning Mode now shows only the user-facing learning sequence `学习界面`, `模板展示`, `Trace 审计`, and `路径图安全验证`. The previous `打开/绑定` and `截图` entries are no longer separate left-sidebar steps in Learn Mode; their app/window/capture controls are embedded at the top of `学习界面` and reuse the same safe open/bind/capture APIs.

Latest trace bug fixed: Learning Draft generation no longer clears the just-created observe response before building `observation_evidence`. Existing Apple Music observe trace replay now produces `raw_screen_inventory=18`, `regions=17`, `page_details.review_only_regions=17`, and `action_templates=0`. This is the intended review-only state before precise calibration: the panel can show interface details and bbox-backed draft regions, but no Execute action is authorized.

Safety pre-run detector follow-up completed during verification: calibration pre-run checks no longer treat Windows `svchost.exe` / `GraphicsPerfSvc` as a model process because of the short `phi` token, while real model hosts such as `llama-server.exe` and `vista_openai_server.py` still block the pre-run as intended.

Next useful work: connect the unified progress states to richer backend status events when the rebuilt recognition/calibration module is ready, and make the manual-edit form apply structured edits to selected regions/actions instead of only recording the edit intent.

2026-07-06 full-screen understanding overlay checkpoint completed. `scripts\run_numbered_region_calibration_probe.py` now writes `full_screen_understanding_summary`, `full_screen_understanding_overlay_path`, and `numbered_region_calibration_backlog_v1` in addition to the selected-region `compiled_overlay_path`. The new full overlay draws every locator card from screen understanding, then overlays Execute dry-run calibration status and points only for calibrated regions. The backlog lists uncalibrated locator cards with region number, label, bbox, suggested semantic action, original locator prompt, and no-execute/no-authorization flags. It now includes actionability triage: interactive-looking regions enter `ready_for_execute_dry_run`, while page-structure/placeholder/count/indicator regions enter `review_before_calibration`. `scripts\report_learn_precise_understanding_fusion_status.py` preserves both the full overlay path and backlog, `load_learning_draft_review()` exposes them through `screen_understanding_preview`, and the Learning Draft panel renders a `View full-screen understanding overlay / 查看全图识别图` button plus a read-only calibration backlog list and `backlog_ready/backlog_review` chips. `scripts\build_numbered_region_calibration_batch_plan.py` now converts that backlog into an offline next-batch plan without executing it. Current plan: `logs\benchmarks\numbered_region_calibration_batch_plan_seek_20260706\numbered_region_calibration_batch_plan.json`, ready regions `1,2,3,6,8,9`, review-blocked regions `7,10`, `command_executes_now=false`, and no `--start-model` flag. `scripts\attach_learn_precise_understanding_fusion_status.py --calibration-batch-plan` can now carry that plan into the Learning Draft preview, and the panel shows a read-only `Next calibration batch / 下一批校准计划` card with `batch_ready/batch_review` chips and the preview command, but no run button. `scripts\build_learn_fusion_pathgraph_preflight.py --calibration-batch-plan` now also carries the plan into `pending_calibration_batch`, so PathGraph preflight chips show `preflight:pending_calibration_ready=6`, `preflight:pending_calibration_review=2`, and `command_executes_now=false`. The current attached loadable draft with full queue + pending-batch preflight is `logs\benchmarks\learn_pathgraph_readiness_with_pending_batch_20260706\actual_parser_output_with_fusion_status.json`. `scripts\report_learn_precise_understanding_readiness.py` now summarizes that draft as `logs\benchmarks\learn_precise_understanding_readiness_20260706\learn_precise_understanding_readiness_report.json`: `readiness_status=needs_pending_calibration`, `calibrated_cases=2/10`, `uncalibrated_locator_cards=8`, `calibration_coverage_rate=0.2`. `load_learning_draft_review()` also exposes that readiness under `screen_understanding_preview.precise_understanding_readiness_summary`, so the panel toolbar shows `readiness=needs_pending_calibration`, `coverage=0.2`, `pending_ready=6`, `pending_review=2`, and `pathgraph=blocked_from_pathgraph_candidate_review`. PathGraph candidate validation now treats this as a hard blocker: pending calibration returns `validation_status=blocked_pending_calibration` with failed check `precise_understanding_ready_for_pathgraph_candidate`, so the pending queue cannot be bypassed by generating a candidate. This prevents a partial calibration probe from being mistaken for a complete full-screen recognition result while still making the whole-image evidence and next calibration queue easy to inspect. Offline preview from existing SEEK artifacts is at `logs\benchmarks\learn_full_screen_understanding_backlog_triage_preview_20260706\full_screen_understanding_backlog_triage_preview_report.json`, with overlay `logs\benchmarks\learn_full_screen_understanding_backlog_triage_preview_20260706\full_screen_understanding_overlay.png`: `total_locator_cards=10`, `calibrated_cases=2`, `uncalibrated_locator_cards=8`, `ready_for_execute_dry_run=6`, `review_before_calibration=2`, `real_clicks=0`. No model was started, no live click/fill/submit was run, and this is still display/review-only evidence, not recognition accuracy, PathGraph promotion, or Execute authorization. Next useful checkpoint is to run the planned dry-run batch only after explicit model-start approval, then merge the new evidence back into the full-screen fusion status.

2026-07-05 fused candidate detail-attachment checkpoint completed. 2026-07-05 SEEK candidate detail-observe attachment checkpoint completed. `scripts\attach_pathgraph_candidate_detail_observe.py` now provides a fixed rerunnable CLI for attaching an existing detail-page learning draft/trial to pending `open_detail` requests in a non-executable `pathgraph_candidate.json`. 2026-07-05 Learning Draft detail-observe attachment UI is now wired. `POST /panel/attach_detail_observe_result` can attach a detail-page learning draft/trial to a pending `open_detail` request in a non-executable PathGraph candidate, and the Learning Draft PathGraph Preview now exposes candidate path / pending request select / request id / recent detail draft select / detail source controls plus a bound attach button. Candidate generation and attachment responses refresh the pending request select; `GET /panel/learning_draft_sources` lists recent loadable learning drafts/trials for the detail source select; selecting either dropdown fills the corresponding input. After attachment, the panel reloads the updated `pathgraph_candidate.json` as the current review source and Page Detail now shows `PathGraph readiness`, `Detail attachments`, attached detail regions, and attached detail actions. `load_learning_draft_review(pathgraph_candidate.json)` returns `pathgraph_candidate_review_v1` with `pathgraph_candidate_readiness_summary_v1` and `pathgraph_promotion_review_gate_v1`, so this attachment evidence and gate status are reloadable rather than only transient UI state. The gate checks current-screen freshness, action taxonomy, verification rules, blockers, final-submit safety, and no-dispatch policy. Freshness demo fixtures now replay matched/missing-file/mismatch branches through the same gate: matched can reach human promotion review, while stale/missing evidence is blocked by `current_screen_freshness`. This is a review-only bridge from list-page open_detail candidates to `model_detail_view`; it does not dispatch clicks, promote a Runtime PathGraph, or authorize Execute. Next checkpoint should apply the same gate to real same-screenshot support captures and prove promotion-blocker behavior on non-demo candidates.

2026-07-05 PathGraph promotion gate replay is now scriptable across existing candidate wrappers. `uv run python scripts\report_pathgraph_promotion_gate_replay.py --candidate-glob "artifacts/learning-draft-review/*/pathgraph_candidate/pathgraph_candidate.json" --out logs\benchmarks\pathgraph_promotion_gate_replay_20260705 --json` produced `logs\benchmarks\pathgraph_promotion_gate_replay_20260705\pathgraph_promotion_gate_replay_report.json`: 17 candidates scanned, 14 non-demo, 3 demo, 1 human-promotion-review branch, 16 blocked. `scripts\bind_pathgraph_candidate_source_freshness.py` then bound existing SEEK source-trial screenshot evidence into a reviewed candidate copy and rebuilt it without screenshot capture or GUI action. The bind report is `logs\benchmarks\pathgraph_candidate_source_freshness_bind_seek_20260705\source_freshness_bind_result.json`; the follow-up replay is `logs\benchmarks\pathgraph_promotion_gate_replay_after_seek_freshness_bind_20260705\pathgraph_promotion_gate_replay_report.json`: 18 candidates scanned, 15 non-demo, 3 demo, 1 non-demo human-promotion-review branch, 1 demo human-promotion-review branch, 16 blocked. The useful signal is not the count; it is that one real non-demo candidate now passes current-screen freshness, while full readiness remains blocked by missing detail attachment / pending observe evidence. Next work should attach or replay the detail observe result for that same SEEK candidate so the readiness blocker moves beyond freshness into actual list/detail fusion.

2026-07-05 numbered-region calibration probe v1 is now runnable. `scripts\run_numbered_region_calibration_probe.py` turns numbered regions into `seeded_candidate_v1`, calls the real Execute dry-run locator chain, and writes a layered report plus full-screen overlay. The baseline SEEK run is `logs\benchmarks\numbered_region_calibration_probe_seek_20260705_v2\numbered_region_calibration_report.json`, overlay `logs\benchmarks\numbered_region_calibration_probe_seek_20260705_v2\numbered_region_calibration_overlay.png`, with `attempted=5`, `passed=2`, `needs_human_review=3`, `real_clicks=0`. The detailed-prompt follow-up is `logs\benchmarks\numbered_region_calibration_probe_seek_enriched_20260705\numbered_region_calibration_report.json`, overlay `logs\benchmarks\numbered_region_calibration_probe_seek_enriched_20260705\numbered_region_calibration_overlay.png`, with `attempted=5`, `passed=2`, `needs_human_review=2`, `gate_rejected=1`, `real_clicks=0`. The important finding is not the count; it is the failure diagnosis. Enriched locator prompts were successfully passed into Execute Mode, but the parser output still lacks enough target detail for semantic regions: Job card text is too generic, Location lacks same-screenshot UIA/OCR/support evidence, and Search keyword still exposes model/seed disagreement. Next checkpoint should change the Learn Mode screen-understanding contract itself: each numbered region must produce a locator task card with real visible text, OCR lines, child elements, neighbor anchors, target boundary definition, and clickable-area rules before Execute dry-run calibration. Do not promote semantic-only seeds, and do not treat either probe as recognition accuracy.

2026-07-05 follow-up completed: Learn Recognition now has `learn_locator_task_cards_v1` as a formal display-only intermediate artifact. Offline replay on the old SEEK observe bundle produced 14 cards at `logs\benchmarks\learn_recognition_locator_task_cards_seek_replay_20260705_v2\locator_task_card_replay_report.json`, but old Job card cards still have empty `text_lines`; this is expected because the saved model output did not contain those anchors. Fresh fixed-screenshot actual parser smoke with the updated prompt is now available at `logs\benchmarks\learn_recognition_locator_task_cards_fresh_qwen8b_20260705_v2\learn_actual_parser_smoke_report.json`; it produced `locator_task_card_count=10` and the Job card cards now include title/company/location text lines. Do not claim recognition accuracy or PathGraph readiness from locator-card richness alone.

2026-07-05 Execute chain probe update: numbered-region seeds have now been tested through the real `/action/execute_recognition_plan` dry-run path with `local_grounding` / VISTA-4B, candidate ranking, narrow search, and pre-click gate. Report: `logs\benchmarks\numbered_region_execute_chain_20260705\numbered_region_execute_chain_report.json`. This confirms the numbered-region idea can enter the Execute locator chain, but it also exposed two runtime issues. `Pay filter` was fixed as a scoped danger false positive: salary/filter controls are no longer blocked just because they contain the token `pay`, while actual payment/submit/send actions remain blocked. The remaining important issue is `Search keyword field`: when VISTA's point disagrees with the seeded bbox, the current system may fall back to the seed center and still allow dry-run. Next checkpoint should tighten that gate or add a numbered-region strict mode so model disagreement is surfaced as review-needed instead of being silently accepted. Do not treat this probe as recognition accuracy or learning-mode reliability.

New 2026-07-04 checkpoint: Learn Recognition now has a first `layout_cleanup` slice between raw parser inventory and classifier / draft generation. It merges duplicate same-target boxes, suppresses large semantic-only containers over concrete child actions, and writes `learn_layout_cleanup_report_v1` into actual parser smoke outputs. A first `Learn Layout Graph v1` slice is now in place too: cleaned candidates become display-only nodes grouped into zones, parent/child containment, reading order, and split-ROI overlap clusters; pipeline results, learning-draft page details, parser smoke outputs, and batch reports expose the graph or its summary. The reviewer-required `Grounding Eligibility Gate v1` is also in place: cleaned candidates now receive `evidence_strength`, `grounding_eligible`, `review_only`, `grounding_block_reason`, and `eligible_for`; smoke and batch reports expose `grounding_eligibility_gate` / `grounding_eligibility_gate_summary` plus `non_actionable_leaked_to_grounding`. The first P1 surface/split diagnostics are now wired too: `browser_chrome_rejection` blocks address-bar / toolbar / tab / extension controls from page ROI grounding, and `split_roi_required` flags overlapping distinct eligible targets that need a smaller ROI split. This is the first code-level step toward the researched OmniParser/UIED-style pipeline: raw candidates -> bbox cleanup -> layout graph / eligibility gate -> ROI grounding -> verifier -> display-only learning draft. It is not a model-accuracy claim and not Execute authorization.

Batch reporting follow-up: actual parser batch output now includes `layout_cleanup_summary` plus per-case `case_results[*].layout_cleanup`, so future fixed-screenshot parser experiments should review cleanup reason distribution before interpreting candidate counts. Panel follow-up completed: Learning Draft PathGraph Preview now renders the `Pipeline audit` card in Page Detail. Next UI work should focus on screenshot/overlay inspection for these same audit items, not on making the draft executable.

First screenshot-inspection slice completed and extended: draft Page Detail list items with bbox now get a `Preview box` action that loads the source screenshot into the existing preview pane, draws the bbox overlay, and opens a full-original-image inspector with the same bbox / point overlaid. Learning Draft main `regions` / `action_templates` now also expose `Edit box` when a source screenshot is available; the inspector supports drag-selecting a new bbox and writing `region_bbox_updates` / `action_bbox_updates` into the reviewed candidate save path. Manual bbox edits now remain auditable: the reviewed candidate stores previous/updated bbox and click point, the panel renders an `edited bbox` badge plus before/after geometry, and `manual_bbox_edit_summary_v1` summarizes edited region/action counts, point-inside-bbox pass/fail, invalid geometry, and non-authorization flags. The same summary now flows into generated PathGraph candidate wrapper and promotion validation report. Screenshot/source freshness warnings are now part of the same audit path through `source_freshness_summary_v1`: missing source image, missing checksum, missing file, and checksum mismatch are visible in the panel, wrapper, and report while Execute binding remains disabled. Offline demo samples for matched, missing-file, and checksum-mismatch branches can now be generated with `uv run python scripts\create_learning_draft_freshness_demo_fixtures.py --json`, and the Learning Draft Review panel has a `Load freshness demo` affordance for loading them. Same-screenshot support acquisition is now queued in `logs\benchmarks\learn_recognition_support_acquisition_queue_next\support_acquisition_queue.json`; preflight says all 4 tasks are ready (`preflight_ready_count=4`, `preflight_blocked_count=0`). This readiness only means target screenshot files/checksums/scripts/manifest are available. The P1 `capture_same_screenshot_support` tasks still require manually reproducing the target window state and then capturing the current bound window; `capture_learn_recognition_same_screenshot_support.py` does not consume the saved screenshot as input. Queue entries now include `support_validation_commands` for `bind_learn_recognition_support_to_manifest.py --validate-only --json`, so support can be checked before writing any manifest. `support_acquisition_status.json` currently shows 1 validated existing support artifact and 3 pending support captures. `next_support_acquisition_brief.json` selects `python_homepage_full_observe_20260703` as the next P1 target and records the reproduce-window -> capture -> validate-only -> bind sequence. Queue bind commands then write per-case manifests under `artifacts\benchmarks\learn_recognition_support_repair\<case_id>\learn_recognition_actual_parser_cases_with_support.json` instead of overwriting the baseline manifest. The next substantial work should execute that brief under the same-window constraint, run validate-only, bind the support only if `bindable=true`, then rerun parser batch + readiness diagnosis before any PathGraph candidate claim.

Completed a small Learning Studio cleanup checkpoint: starting screen understanding or draft generation now forces the Learn Replay subview to `draft` and clears stale template PathGraph/page-detail displays before new evidence is shown. Follow-up tightening is in place: entering Learn Fast no longer reopens the shared PathGraph surface after cleanup, and selecting the learning-draft subview actively hides shared PathGraph/Page Detail state. Next P1 evidence-acquisition path is scaffolded: use `scripts\capture_learn_recognition_same_screenshot_support.py` after binding the target window to capture a fresh framework screenshot plus same-window UIA snapshot into `learn_recognition_same_screenshot_support_v1`. 2026-07-05 checkpoint: `parser_candidate_v1` is now the common pluggable-parser evidence projection attached to every Learn Recognition inventory item. Treat this as the Support Eligibility Gate input contract: Qwen/vision semantic regions and OCR-only anchors remain review-only unless same-screenshot UIA/OmniParser/calibrated/no-dispatch Execute support proves interactivity. Next work should acquire or bind more same-screenshot support for the 4 blocked actual parser cases and then rerun readiness diagnostics; do not promote semantic-only regions into PathGraph candidate review.

2026-07-05 follow-up: the offline benchmark now reports `support_eligibility_summary` from `parser_candidate_v1`. 2026-07-05 update: support eligibility is now a shared runtime/reporting module and is also emitted by actual parser smoke/batch reports. 2026-07-05 repair-target update: `scripts\report_learn_recognition_pathgraph_readiness_diagnosis.py` now emits `support_repair_targets`. 2026-07-05 bbox-alignment update: support repair diagnosis now includes `bbox_alignment_audit` using the same overlap thresholds as parser cross-evidence. 2026-07-05 calibrated-support probe update: one locked target now has exact-SHA reviewed support. `scripts\create_learn_recognition_calibrated_support.py` generated `artifacts\benchmarks\learn_recognition_support_repair\python_homepage_saved_template_screenshot\same_screenshot_calibrated_support.json` for `python_homepage_saved_template_screenshot`. Rerun report `logs\benchmarks\learn_recognition_support_repair_python_saved_search_coordinate_diagnosis_v1\learn_pathgraph_readiness_blocker_diagnosis_report.json` changes that target to `matching_interactable_support_found`, but `bbox_alignment_audit.status=support_found_but_bbox_alignment_failed` with `attempted=2`, `passed=0`, and best-match IoU `0.0`. The new coordinate diagnosis classifies both mismatches as `raw_model_bbox_misaligned_before_remap`, not coordinate-remap failure: raw Qwen coordinates are already wrong in the 1280x660 inference image before restore to the original screenshot. Treat this as a successful diagnostic, not a successful recognition case. The next smallest useful step is to run an alternative parser/ROI locator or tighten the parser prompt/model contract on this exact case, then require bbox alignment to pass before PathGraph candidate promotion.

The safe binding step is also scaffolded: use `scripts\bind_learn_recognition_support_to_manifest.py` to create a new manifest with `supplemental_sources_path` only when the support artifact checksum exactly matches the target case screenshot. The binder rejects stale support and does not mutate the original manifest. Recommended order:

```powershell
uv run python scripts\capture_learn_recognition_same_screenshot_support.py `
  --out artifacts\benchmarks\learn_recognition_support\<case_id> `
  --app-name <app> `
  --state-hint <state> `
  --json

uv run python scripts\bind_learn_recognition_support_to_manifest.py `
  --manifest artifacts\benchmarks\learn_recognition_actual_parser_cases_v1.json `
  --case-id <case_id> `
  --support artifacts\benchmarks\learn_recognition_support\<case_id>\same_screenshot_uia_support.json `
  --out artifacts\benchmarks\learn_recognition_actual_parser_cases_with_support_v1.json `
  --json
```

Also fixed the grounding matrix report semantics key so per-profile bbox checks use the model profile id from the batch report, not a temporary profile JSON path.

## Recommended Next Step

The next stage is the actual parser smoke/matrix for Learn Recognition. Execute Mode, Operation, Gate, Trace, and final-submit safety remain preserved; the work is to prove whether fixed screenshots can produce parser outputs that are useful for review and, separately, useful for grounding.

Immediate next implementation target:

0.1. Completed: add `Grounding Eligibility Gate v1` before ROI grounding. Keep `grounding_eligible=true` scoped only to ROI grounding eligibility, not click authorization, Execute readiness, or PathGraph promotion.

0.15. Completed: add `Learn Layout Graph v1` after cleanup. Keep it display-only and use it to feed page details, parser smoke artifacts, batch summaries, and future raw/cleaned/eligible overlay work.

0.2. Extend layout cleanup and eligibility from the current duplicate/container slice into a measurable resolver benchmark:
   - compare raw vs cleaned overlay counts on saved python.org / SEEK screenshots;
   - add source-specific priority diagnostics for UIA / OmniParser / calibrated target / vision / OCR;
   - expose raw candidates vs cleaned candidates in the Learning Draft panel so demos can show what the system removed and why;
   - keep every cleanup decision reviewable and non-authorizing.

0.3. Completed as a minimum diagnostic slice: add `chrome/page separation` and `split_roi_required` diagnostics behind the same eligibility gate. Next, validate these fields on broader fixed screenshots and expose raw / cleaned / eligible / split-ROI overlays in the Learning Draft panel. Do not prioritize more model downloads or prompt trials until non-actionable leakage remains zero on a broader fixture set.

0. For the 4 blocked PathGraph readiness cases, attach same-screenshot OCR/UIA/OmniParser/calibrated-target support or prove parser bbox alignment before wiring them into PathGraph candidate review. Use `parser_candidate_v1` as the normalized evidence shape and keep `semantic_region` / `ocr_text_anchor` review-only until an interactable source exists. Do not wire semantic-only parser regions directly into PathGraph.

1. Add or harden actual parser smoke report fields:
   - `parser_inventory_generated`
   - `parser_useful_for_review`
   - `parser_useful_for_grounding`
   - `semantic_only_regions`
   - `grounding_eligible_regions`
   - `accepted_for_grounding`
   - `blocked_from_grounding_reason`
2. Run fixed-screenshot actual parser cases before more grounding model trials:
   - python.org homepage
   - SEEK results
   - SEEK detail
   - form page
   - docs/code page
   - modal/popup
   - wrong surface
3. Keep semantic-only Qwen regions as review-only unless OCR/UIA/DOM/parser interactable cross-evidence makes them grounding eligible.
4. Only after parser actual-call evidence exists, run parser -> classifier -> ROI -> grounding -> validator -> `learning_template_draft_v1` -> PathGraph candidate on at least 10 cases.

The broader Learn Recognition rebuild remains the same. Clean and replace the front half of Learn Mode recognition while keeping the existing Learning Draft Review, DraftGraph Preview, PathGraph candidate, and promotion-validation display pipeline.

28.17. Completed: UGround 2B was rechecked as the current low-risk actual-call grounding profile. The current materialization report `logs\benchmarks\learn_recognition_model_materialization_uground2b_current\materialization_report.json` reports `materialization_status=materialized_candidate` and `blockers=[]` for the smoke-verified launchable profile. The fixed matrix rerun at `logs\benchmarks\learn_recognition_grounding_model_matrix_uground2b_current\learn_grounding_model_matrix_report.json` made 9 fresh UGround 2B ROI grounding calls and kept 3 precondition stops blocked. Although all 9 calls passed Validator, the new `point_center_bias_diagnostic` reports `center_bias_risk` (`near_center_outputs=7/9`, `exact_center_outputs=6/9`), so this must not be treated as recognition reliability or 90% accuracy evidence.
28.18. Completed: the fixed grounding matrix now includes 3 off-center ROI hard cases through benchmark-only `roi_bbox_override`. The candidate set has 15 cases: 12 actual-call candidates and 3 precondition stops. Latest UGround 2B report: `logs\benchmarks\learn_recognition_grounding_model_matrix_uground2b_offcenter\learn_grounding_model_matrix_report.json`. UGround 2B passed 11/12 actual calls and failed the python.org off-center Search button with `model_point_outside_roi_candidate_bbox`; `point_center_bias_diagnostic` still reports `center_bias_risk` (`near_center_outputs=8/12`, `exact_center_outputs=7/12`). This is useful failure evidence, not a 90% claim.
28.19. Completed: VISTA baseline and UGround 2B now have a same-run comparison on the 15-case off-center matrix. Report: `logs\benchmarks\learn_recognition_grounding_model_matrix_vista_uground2b_offcenter\learn_grounding_model_matrix_report.json`. VISTA passed 8/12 fresh actual calls with 4 `model_point_outside_roi_candidate_bbox` failures; UGround 2B passed 11/12 with 1 same-category failure but keeps `center_bias_risk`. Case-level diagnosis `logs\benchmarks\learn_recognition_grounding_case_diagnosis_vista_vs_uground2b_offcenter\learn_grounding_case_diagnosis_report.json` shows VISTA failures are real model point-quality misses on SEEK search button / Pay filter with valid ROI, coordinate transform, screenshot freshness, and Validator rejection. UGround 2B passed those same cases, but this still does not prove reliability.
28.20. Completed: the candidate set has been expanded again from 15 to 19 cases, with 16 fresh actual-call candidates and 3 precondition stops. New hard cases cover a wide SEEK search-input off-center ROI, a python.org Downloads/Documentation multi-candidate nav crop, and python.org Get Started/Download dual-button crop. Latest report: `logs\benchmarks\learn_recognition_grounding_model_matrix_vista_uground2b_hardcases\learn_grounding_model_matrix_report.json`. VISTA passed 11/16 and UGround 2B passed 12/16; both failed only by `model_point_outside_roi_candidate_bbox`, and both kept no-action safety. Diagnosis reports: `logs\benchmarks\learn_recognition_grounding_case_diagnosis_vista_vs_uground2b_hardcases\learn_grounding_case_diagnosis_report.json` and `logs\benchmarks\learn_recognition_grounding_case_diagnosis_uground2b_vs_vista_hardcases\learn_grounding_case_diagnosis_report.json`. The key new finding is that UGround 2B fails multi-candidate label disambiguation on python.org crops where VISTA passes, while UGround still beats VISTA on SEEK small controls.
29. Next: add cross-screenshot hard cases before any profile promotion and consider an ensemble/validator strategy instead of a single-profile replacement. Specifically add more multi-candidate same-crop samples, wrong-label hard negatives, intentionally off-center target boxes, and independent SEEK/python screenshots. For actual parser support, add real same-screenshot OCR/UIA/OmniParser/calibrated-target support for the currently review-only python and SEEK cases instead of reusing stale support. Only after failures/successes, center-bias diagnostics, and label-disambiguation errors are separated across independent screenshots should we consider UGround 7B materialization for quality comparison. Do not lower Validator, do not count recorded evidence as actual model ability, and do not promote any profile based on this matrix.

The 90% target must be measured as layered Learn Recognition metrics, not a single accuracy claim. The first benchmark must separate `parse_inventory`, `actionable_classification`, `non_actionable_rejection`, `grounding_point`, `coordinate_transform`, `danger_zone_rejection`, and `pathgraph_candidate_validation`, with `not_covered` when attempted is zero.

Legacy final-package notes below remain useful as safety context, but they are no longer the active next step.

## Previous Final Package Guidance

The prior stage was ready for Checkpoint 7 final-package review. Do not add new runtime features, do not rerun live SEEK, and do not tune prompts before the reviewer accepts the final package. Use `FINAL_DEMO_READINESS_PACKAGE.md` as the interview/demo scaffold and evidence map.

After final-package review, the next technical work should still be evidence-first: add more independent point-grounding fixtures, add more model-generated dev/holdout cases, and expand safe-fill fixtures before any future live single-field smoke.

Architecture-first next step after final review: keep the GUI Agent Runtime reset, but do not continue by tuning the learning prompt first. Use the fixed-fixture model-learning template feedback loop before promoting any model output to a reusable template asset.

Immediate architecture work:

1. Rerun `scripts\run_model_learning_feedback_loop.py` with `model_learning_template_dev_manifest_v1.json` and `model_learning_template_holdout_manifest_v1.json` whenever a model prompt/profile change is proposed. Dev may select parameters; holdout is final evaluation only. Accept only hard-metric improvements with no safety, loader, invalid-fixture, source-tracking, reference-leakage, raw-sensitive-value, or wording regression.
2. Keep `draft_reference_alignment_score` / `template_similarity_score` as raw draft/reference alignment only. Do not call it model accuracy, click success, gate success, or SEEK E2E success.
3. Do not hand-edit generated templates after scoring and count that as model improvement. If deterministic skeleton completion or field normalization is used, mark the result as `assisted_generation`, not pure model output.
3.5. Use the Learn Replay panel's Learning Studio for current-window draft trials, simple review, and candidate generation only. The panel's default `运行草稿试跑` path now calls `POST /panel/run_learning_recognition_trial`, which converts current Learn Fast / Learn Deep evidence into `learn_observe_bundle_v1`, replays calibrated target coordinate evidence through the common Learn Recognition parser/classifier/grounding/validator pipeline, rejects tiny calibrated noise boxes before grounding, and saves a display-only `learn_recognition_trial` under `artifacts\learning-runs\...\trial_result.json`. Recognition drafts now carry display-only `page_details` with screen-understanding summary, read-only/rejected regions, grounding candidates, model roles, inventory counts, and screenshot context so demos can inspect what the learning pass saw without converting those regions into executable actions. The older `POST /panel/run_learning_model_trial` remains only as a compatibility/experimental model-trial endpoint. `POST /panel/generate_pathgraph_candidate` can turn the reviewed draft into `pathgraph_candidate.json` plus runtime graph/interface-map candidate files and a validation report; these candidate artifacts preserve `page_details` as read-only review context and explicitly keep `candidate_only=true` and `execute_binding_enabled=false`. Even `validation_status=passed_candidate` is not promotion or execution authorization. A saved `reviewed_template_candidate.json` remains `mixed` or `assisted_generation`, `display_and_review_only`, and not pure model-generated evidence.
4. Keep the canonical required-field post-validation gate active for every parsed draft. Logical `states`, `regions`, `action_templates`, `safety_policy`, `blockers`, and `verification_rules` must resolve through `learning_template_required_contract_v1`; missing logical fields must set `required_field_retry_needed=true` and emit a `missing_sections_patch` retry plan with target schema paths. Without live `actual_model_call` or compliant `recorded_output_per_config` evidence, the report must record `retry_not_executed` instead of claiming improvement.
5. Before accepting any prompt/config trial, add either more live `actual_model_call` outputs or `recorded_output_per_config` artifacts for that exact trial config. The first Checkpoint 4 actual-call sample ran 3 dev cases with required-field retry evidence, but hard metrics did not improve. Checkpoint 4.6 aligned the field contract and reran the saved actual outputs offline; the remaining hard gaps are `blockers` and `verification_rules`, not a missing `safety_policy` alias. Checkpoint 4.7 tried a targeted missing-section patch retry on those same saved dev cases; all 3 model patch outputs parsed, but validator rejected them for missing `schema_version` and empty/generic blocker/verification sections. Checkpoint 4.8 classified that as `patch_contract_and_grounding_failure` and froze the decision in `logs\benchmarks\model_learning_patch_retry_checkpoint4_8_decision\failure_decision_report.json`. Checkpoint 4.9 recommends the needs-human-review path in `logs\benchmarks\model_learning_patch_retry_checkpoint4_9_path_selection\path_selection_report.json`; assisted generation and one stronger patch-contract prompt experiment remain deferred unless reviewer explicitly chooses one. Treat this as evidence that the current patch prompt is insufficient, not as accepted model improvement. Baseline `recorded_model_output` and non-improving actual calls prove what the model produced, not that a prompt/config should be accepted.
5.5. Checkpoint 5 final validation has been rerun at `logs\benchmarks\model_learning_feedback_loop_final\feedback_report.json`. Holdout remains unused for tuning, leakage/safety audits pass, selected config is baseline, and no trial is accepted for model ability. Do not claim prompt/config improvement from fixture-only runner logic.
5.6. Checkpoint 6 produced one reviewer-approved live no-submit smoke at `logs\smoke\seek_live_no_submit_checkpoint6\checkpoint6_live_smoke_summary.json`. It is useful as a demo trace for opening/capturing SEEK results and extracting cards, but it stopped before detail/apply because the single visible job was skipped by prefilter. Do not rerun or expand live behavior for this checkpoint; move to final package unless the reviewer explicitly requests a new bounded smoke.
6. Expand the model-learning dev/holdout manifests only after the current feedback loop is stable. Add independent observe traces/screenshots for list/detail, form inventory, wrong surface, modal, and non-SEEK app templates. Mark every case and generated template as `actual_model_call`, `recorded_model_output`, `recorded_output_per_config`, `assisted_generation`, `human_curated`, `mixed`, or `fixture_only`.
7. Add more independent point-grounding fixtures instead of promoting the current small set. The SEEK manifest now has confirmed-point success, recognition-plan/VISTA success, recognition-plan/VISTA miss, and gate-rejected click evidence, but it still needs more windows/DPI/ROI/candidate variants before any stability trend is meaningful.
8. Keep using `scripts\run_seek_mvp_benchmark.py` as the fixed SEEK rerun command. Reports must output layered `passed / attempted / rate`, with `rate=not_covered` when `attempted=0`, and list failure category / trace path / screenshot path.
9. Add real fixture/golden cases for `model_draft_alignment`, more `full_no_submit_e2e` surfaces, more point-grounding edge cases, and a larger safe-fill fixture matrix. Live `safe_fill` is still `not_covered`; do not run live safe-fill until the profile/readiness and single-field preview gates are explicitly green.
10. Keep the new SEEK state-machine invariant: `external_ats + login_required` must emit `safe_stop` and must not continue SEEK card loops, result extraction, result scrolling, or next-card lookup.
9. Keep final-submit guard fixture coverage green for submit wording, disabled submit, sticky footer submit, and modal submit before any live apply-form test.
10. Continue splitting reusable Operation skills from SEEK-specific runners and keep Gate common/profile-driven: action taxonomy, candidate freshness, scroll scope, scoped danger detection, final submit blocking, and profile mutation approval should remain reusable.

Immediate next speed work:

1. Keep the new `Quick apply` visual-asset fast path: current screenshot -> `seek:job_detail` header ROI -> learned button crop match -> fresh confirmed-point candidate -> `/action/execute_confirmed_point`. Do not reintroduce a required OCR `Quick apply` precondition before the visual match.
2. Move application-flow steps to learned region batch reads. `continue_application_flow` still costs about `30s` on the first application page transition; it should read the current application step region and question area directly instead of running broad observe-style checks.
3. Reduce `extract_cards` and `read_detail_batch` latency. Current real checkpoint costs are roughly `extract_cards=8-11s`, `read_detail_batch=9-15s`, `execute_apply_entry=18.5s`, and first `continue_application_flow=30.5s`.

Record the URL after the click, classify external account/privacy/login/captcha/upload surfaces as user-review stop points, and keep only `Quick apply` on the station-internal path.
5. Keep final submit/send/confirm/payment hard-blocked. The new speed work may remove duplicate low-risk confirmations, but must not bypass scoped final-submit detection or user/GPT review for risky employer questions.

Current SEEK execution tuning keeps the simplified key-gate runner path, but learned-fast mode must not perform local keyword pruning. Low-risk job-card opening should not run an outer `dry_run_card`, and Apply Entry should not run an outer `dry_run_apply_entry`; the execute steps already create/reuse approved plans internally. Speed demo uses `execute_card --fast-open-detail`, which may open a learned job card with `/action/execute_confirmed_point` from the current extracted `card_bbox / click_point` instead of VISTA grounding, then lets `read_detail_batch` confirm the detail pane through the known `seek:job_detail` region. Every card that is attempted must reach complete detail reading and Agent suitability judgment; senior/specialist/high-experience words cannot cause a local skip. Final-review extraction should run only after the flow reaches `review_and_submit` / scoped final-submit-visible evidence. Keep strong checks at Quick Apply entry, application-flow navigation, safe field fill, unresolved employer questions, and final submit / send / confirm / payment boundaries. Employer-question fill should use partial-ready execution: fill evidence-backed low-risk answers first, record unresolved or sensitive questions in `blocked_questions`, and stop for review instead of blocking the whole page up front.

Final submit remediation is now the first gate before any future live apply run. The 2026-06-24 SEEK submit showed that thread-level intent and a broad active goal are not enough to decide whether a specific job should be submitted. Before any final `Submit application` click, the runner or supervising agent must build `final_submit_decision_v1` / `pre_submit_suitability_audit_v1`, consult GPT or the user for ambiguous `maybe_apply` / location / contract / employer-answer risks, and pass that structured decision through the common final-submit guard. Without that metadata, `POST /action/execute_recognition_plan` must block the click even when `metadata.forbid_final_submit=false`.

Public SEEK search should use `scripts\seek_debug_step_runner.py --step search_keyword_submit --search-query graduate`, which dispatches `/action/type_text` with `submit=true` and records `seek_search_submit_v1`; do not locate and click the search button unless Enter submission fails with trace evidence. `read_detail_batch` locks the known `seek:job_detail` container, captures/OCRs multiple ROI snapshots while scrolling with adaptive wheel clicks, merges unique line hashes, and stops after repeated no-new-content or wrong-scope/boundary evidence. The debug runner now recommends `read_detail_batch` as the normal post-`verify_detail` path; `read_detail_scroll` is retained as a single-scroll diagnostic. Apply Entry no longer waits a fixed 6 seconds before the after screenshot: `seek_application_flow_wait_v1` reuses the application-flow state already returned by the Apply step, and only polls whole-screen observe up to the 3-second default maximum when that state is unclear. Apply Entry dry-run should also reuse a fresh visible Apply / Quick Apply bbox/click point from the latest detail snapshot; reset `seek:job_detail` only when that candidate is missing or verification cannot see Apply. Trace Inspector now renders `visual_asset_recall_v1` as a dedicated `Visual Assets` stage and previews the learned template, current ROI crop, and current match crop. No-mouse local visual smoke now exists for this path: `uv run python scripts\visual_asset_local_smoke.py --out-dir artifacts\visual-match-smoke\local_seek_buttons`. It generates a local SEEK-like screenshot, exports Apply / Quick apply crops from learned visual-control samples, adds a high-risk Submit application crop, matches Quick apply from the crop without touching any real window or mouse, verifies Submit application remains `potential_side_effect_action` with `final_submit_fast_lane_count=0`, and merges current match evidence back into `learned_interface_map_v1`. The current calibration report shows Quick Apply screenshot recall in about `9ms`, high-risk Submit evidence recall in about `11ms`, and zero final-submit fast-lane authorization. A duplicate-button regression also proves that similar low-risk buttons are blocked from fast lane when the top1/top2 score gap is too small. Use this while the user is using the computer. The same smoke now writes `learned_interface_map_v1` to `artifacts\visual-match-smoke\local_seek_buttons\learned_interface_map.json`; this is the clean data contract for the redesigned Learn Mode panel. The reusable CLI `scripts\visual_asset_calibration_report.py` can now take that interface map plus any current screenshot and emit `visual_asset_calibration_report_v1` plus an updated map, so live SEEK calibration does not need to duplicate the smoke script. Default source-bbox calibration ROI is intentionally tight; broad matching must come from explicit region scope to avoid adjacent-button ambiguity. The Learn Replay page can now load both the interface map and a separate calibration report into a large workbench instead of a flat list: a state-flow strip shows page states, a calibration strip shows current match/fast-lane/high-risk timing counters, region lanes group fixed visual assets / dynamic ROI areas / danger zones, fixed visual nodes show crop thumbnails and calibration status, and the sticky Inspector exposes source crop/current ROI/current match/current bbox/current click point/scope/risk/raw JSON evidence. The Inspector can also recrop a fixed button from its source screenshot and bbox through `/panel/crop_interface_asset`; this updates the current map evidence but keeps fast lane disabled until the next calibration. Operator edits still save through `/panel/save_interface_map` with `learned_interface_map_edit_trace_v1`. The next UI implementation step is explicit edit history and region bbox overlay editing, followed by live SEEK visual-asset calibration now that real mouse/window testing is allowed again.

Live SEEK visual calibration has now been proven on a real maximized Edge window. The old synthetic `Quick apply` crop did not match the live detail pane, so the next Learn Mode change should promote real observed button crops into the interface map during Learn Fast/Deep and treat synthetic crops only as tests. The live-cropped `Quick apply` asset matched the current detail screenshot in `24.817ms`, entered the low-risk `open_apply_flow` fast-lane candidate path, and opened the `Choose documents` page without final submit. Next implementation work should make this automatic: after Learn Mode detects a stable fixed control, crop tight/context images, store them with region scope and negative final-submit text, then require current-capture calibration before Execute can use the candidate.

The broader SEEK job-search/application experience is now frozen as reusable guidance in `artifacts\templates\job_search_application_workflow_template_v1.json` and `artifacts\skills\job_search_application_workflow_skill_v1.json`. This is not an auto-apply permission layer. It composes job/company recording, detail reading, profile screening, same-site nonfinal application entry, multi-step form filling, Review reconciliation, and final-submit blocking into a one-step-at-a-time upper-agent workflow. Next generic validation should add a local non-SEEK job-board fixture with at least one strong same-site internal application, one skipped or needs-review job, and one external ATS sample that is deferred.

The path-graph-assisted Execute API can now do one-step low-level dispatch: `POST /execute/available_actions` emits `available_actions_response_v1`, and `POST /execute/step` emits `execute_step_response_v1` with `path_graph_action_context_v1` plus `path_graph_runtime_state_v1`. With `dispatch_low_level=true`, `/execute/step` dispatches exactly one generated request through the existing gated `/action/scroll`, `/action/execute_recognition_plan`, or `/action/type_text` route and records the low-level response/trace. The panel now places learned-artifact replay in the owning workspace: Learn Mode shows Artifact Replay and PathGraph Safe Validation, while Execute Mode shows PathGraph Task Run. Switching workspaces hides the other workspace's functions but keeps Session/System tools visible. Input is visible in the skill matrix as dry-run-only unless a safety policy explicitly allows live input.

The current button-level panel workflow is documented in `docs/PANEL_LEARN_EXECUTE_WORKFLOW.zh-CN.md`. Use it as the operator checklist for manual smoke runs: bind a window, run Learn Fast / Learn Deep, load or validate a learned artifact, then switch to Execute Mode and step through available actions or task-run replay one backend step at a time.

Runtime storage note: if the workspace grows unexpectedly again, inspect `logs\cleanup\cleanup_manifest_20260620_v2.json`, `logs\cleanup\cleanup_manifest_20260620_v3.json`, and the largest files under `logs\traces` before deleting dependencies. `write_trace` now has a shared budget guard, so new GB-scale scroll traces should be treated as a regression. Do not remove `models`, `.venv`, `tools`, SEEK milestone JSON artifacts, templates, or skills unless that scope is explicitly approved.

For future station-internal SEEK application-fill tests, use the reusable final-review audit instead of a hand-written checklist:

```powershell
uv run python scripts\seek_application_final_review_audit.py `
  --record logs\smoke\seek_application_fill_<job_id>\application_fill_record.json `
  --fail-on-error
```

The run is not complete unless the audit emits `decision=pass_stopped_before_final_submit` and proves `final_submissions=0`, `submit_clicks=0`, `cover_letter_filled=true`, employer questions answered from evidence, `persistent_profile_updates=0`, `seek_profile_suggestions_choice="Don't include"` when suggestions are shown or `"not_shown"` when no suggestion choice appears, and screenshot/trace evidence for the final Review boundary.

After a reviewed stop-before-submit run passes audit, freeze it as non-authorizing Learn Mode evidence:

```powershell
uv run python scripts\seek_export_application_flow_artifact.py `
  --record logs\smoke\seek_application_fill_<job_id>\application_fill_record.json `
  --audit logs\smoke\seek_application_fill_<job_id>\final_review_audit.json `
  --out artifacts\seek\learned_seek_application_flow_<job_id>.json
```

Current example: `artifacts\seek\learned_seek_application_flow_20260620.json` from `logs\smoke\seek_debug_homepage_20260620`. Treat `seek_application_flow_artifact_v1` as milestone evidence only; future replay still needs safe-fill focus/value verification and final submit remains forbidden. The safe-fill focus path now requires the dry-run selected focus point to be inside the target field bbox before any real focus click is reused; `safe_field_focus_point_outside_field_bbox` is a hard stop, not a fallback-to-type condition. The one-step debug runner now consumes `logs\smoke\seek_application_flow_replay_20260620.json` through `--application-flow-replay`, so every `continue_application_flow` report should include the selected `seek_apply:*` transition and its screenshot/safe-fill verification requirements.

Before rerunning live SEEK agent-like tests, keep the browser on a fresh search-results page and verify `seek:results_list` is inferred from visible job-card bboxes, not from a hard-coded left/right layout. If a run starts from the footer or an empty page, it should stop with `blocked_no_initial_job_cards` instead of scrolling. Use `scripts\stop_seek_mvp_runner.ps1` if a local SEEK traversal process needs to be stopped.

For external-agent SEEK tests, require the agent to keep `/apps/open.maximize_after_open=true` and inspect the open-app trace for `maximize_error=null` before Observe. Current timing evidence shows the next speed win is not mouse dispatch: real click reuse is about `1.9s` and scroll is about `1.7s`; repeated `Observe/screen_reading` and oversized VISTA ROI calls dominate. The runtime now treats `seeded_candidate_v1` as the primary compressed VISTA path (`compact_seed_candidate_roi_primary`), while ambiguous union crops and full-screen VISTA direct grounding are fallback paths. Optimize the agent loop by observing once per stable page state, reading details from the already-known `seek:job_detail` container, and sending extracted card bboxes / `seeded_candidate_v1` for same-layout job-card clicks instead of rerunning full discovery for each card.

The latest short live SEEK calibration (`logs\smoke\seek_first_real_job_adaptive_scroll_20260619.json`) verified the primary fixes for the current bug report: real click dispatch now waits `200ms` after mouse move before clicking, the first real job card opened successfully, `/action/scroll` receives the results/detail container bbox in API `width/height` format, and detail scroll strength escalated as `[4, 8, 12]`. After a card opens, detail reading should use the already-known right-pane container (`fixed_seek_job_detail_container_after_card_click`) instead of another precise target relocation. `seek_job_match_decision_v1` now carries `fit_summary` and `recommended_next_action`; for `strong_apply`, the recommended next action is opening Apply Entry and preparing safe fields. It was intentionally limited to `--max-detail-scrolls 3`, so it did not prove a complete detail read. Next live regression should run with `--max-detail-scrolls 8` or higher and confirm `jobs_fully_read>=1` before returning to the full 5-job smoke.

During debug, do not run SEEK traversal as a long unattended script. Use `scripts\seek_debug_step_runner.py`, which performs one bounded step, writes `logs\smoke\seek_debug_step_run_latest\state.json`, creates `step_NNN_<step>\step_report.json`, records screenshot paths, and exits. Recommended sequence from the SEEK homepage: `--step open --allow-close-windows`, `--step bind_and_resize_verify`, `--step search_keyword_submit --search-query graduate`, `--step extract_cards`, `--step dry_run_card --job-index 0`, `--step execute_card --job-index 0`, `--step verify_detail`, `--step read_detail_batch`, then `--step match`. Use `read_detail_scroll` only when debugging one scroll event. Continue only after inspecting the previous step report screenshots. The debug open step requests a separate browser window with `--new-window`; the close step skips multi-tab browser windows instead of closing unrelated tabs. The bind/resize step records `seek_bound_window_verification_v1` and `coordinate_window_size` before any card click. The detail-scroll step sends the current `seek:job_detail` bbox to `/action/scroll` and records `right_detail_scroll_validation_v1`; if the right-detail fingerprint is unchanged, stop instead of sending more wheel events that could bleed into the left results list. The same validation now records a screenshot-crop based `left_results_visual_stability` signal and uses it ahead of noisy semantic card fingerprints when deciding whether the left results pane stayed stable.

## Learning Modes Plan

Detailed Chinese plan: `LEARNING_MODE_PLAN.zh-CN.md`.

Split learning into two first-class modes:

1. `exploration_learning`
   - Purpose: actively discover an app or page state space without a specific one-click user goal.
   - Start as dry-run only.
   - Later allow only low-risk real clicks with depth limits, blacklists, state dedupe, backtracking, and strict verification.
   - Output should be an interface/state action map, not a directly reusable instruction.

2. `path_recording_learning`
   - Purpose: passively record every real gated click as a path event with before/after state, selected point, gate decision, traces, and verification.
   - This mode does not choose actions by itself; it records actions that already passed the runtime action boundary.
   - Output should be `path_click_event_v1` entries and a deterministic `runtime_path_graph_v1`.
   - Stable verified path events can later be promoted into `learned_instruction_v1`.

`instruction_learning` should be treated as a promoted artifact from verified path records, not as a separate unrestricted third mode. Reuse may skip model inference, but it must still validate the current goal/window/state and run real post-click verification.

First MVP slice starts in Observe: `POST /vision/observe_screen` returns `screen_map_v1`, and the browser panel's existing navigation-path card consumes `screen_map.sections` plus `screen_map.candidates` so whole-screen understanding immediately produces a sectioned page/action map. Observe traces also preserve the map, and `/panel/inspect_trace` renders it as a `Path Map` stage with path candidates and overlay evidence. These bbox and click-point hints remain observation evidence only; precise localization and gated execution still happen later.

Immediate smoke target:

```text
MouseTester.cn
goal: 鐐瑰嚮姝ゅ娴嬭瘯
run 0: observe_screen, verify screen_map_v1 appears, `sections` separate top navigation/main content/floating overlays, the panel path card shows candidates with section ids, and Trace Inspector shows the observe trace `Path Map` stage
run 1: execute_recognition_plan with a real verified click, write path_click_event_v1
verify: artifacts/local-learning/path-runs/{run_id}/ contains path_graph.json, event JSON, before/after screenshots, diff image, traces, and target crop
run 2: promote the verified path event into learned_instruction_v1
run 3: execute_recognition_plan with learned_instruction_id, verify the same point is reused without a recognition_plan model pass
verify: the desktop response path graph shows both the path event and learning-asset bundle node
```

0. Keep the upper-layer agent API workflow explicit and testable.
   - Required doc: `AGENT_API_WORKFLOW.md`
   - Live sequence: prepare dependencies with `POST /runtime/prepare`, discover apps with `GET /apps`, optionally open software with `POST /apps/open`, bind a window, optionally type through `POST /action/type_text`, observe the whole screen with `POST /vision/observe_screen`, precisely locate a chosen target with `POST /vision/locate_target`, dry-run `POST /action/execute_recognition_plan`, then execute only if `pre_click_decision_v1` allows it
   - The workflow must keep OCR anchors in the recognition path and prevent raw model bboxes from becoming click coordinates
   - Keep `POST /action/execute_confirmed_point` limited to the desktop panel's deliberate human-review workflow; autonomous execution must remain on the recognition-plan gate

1. Keep the no-surprises execution route as the action boundary.
   - Route: `POST /action/execute_recognition_plan`
   - Preferred input: live bound-window capture, goal, app name, top-k, verification hints
   - Output: recognition plan, action result, post-click verification result, trace path
   - Saved screenshots are blocked from actual execution unless explicitly overridden, because their coordinates may not match the current window.

2. Build the Single-page stable loop.
   - Use `observe_screen.screen_map` as the semantic map that seeds the browser panel navigation-path card
   - Run the same MouseTester goal repeatedly from a live bound window
   - Save each run as recognition trace, action trace, before/after screenshots, validation summary, and evaluation facts
   - Measure top-1 candidate stability, local OCR match rate, selected click-point drift, pre-click allow/reject stability, action execution, and semantic validation

3. Add path-recording write-back to the recognition execution mainline.
   - When `execute_recognition_plan` performs a real click, write `path_click_event_v1` with the goal, app/site identity, state fingerprint, selected candidate id, bbox/refined bbox, selected/clicked point, UIA/OCR evidence, before/after screenshots, validation result, recognition trace path, and action trace path
   - Generate or update a deterministic `runtime_path_graph_v1` from those events
   - Promote only verified path events into reusable `learned_instruction_v1` records
   - On later runs, allow learned records to guide reuse only after current goal/window/state validation; post-click validation remains mandatory

4. Add cross-session stability cases.
   - Repeat the same task across different sessions and days
   - Vary window size, DPI/display scaling, browser zoom, reload state, and page scroll/focus state
   - Classify failures by layer: OCR, vision regions, page structure, screen reading, candidate ranking, pre-click gate, action dispatch, or validator

5. Expand overlay review.
   - Show original bbox, refined bbox, crop bbox, refined point, and decision reasons
   - Keep screenshots and JSON traces separate from logs

6. Wire stronger UI providers into `screen_reading_v1` incrementally.
   - First candidate: browser accessibility/DOM for web content controls
   - Second candidate: icon shape/template matching for visual-only controls; the previous Fluent catalog shortcut has been removed
   - Later: learned UI memory

7. Measure OCR-anchor impact.
   - Add adaptive prompt-budget retry for dense pages: the Seek/Serato regression showed that default `48` rows can exhaust the `4096` context, so test a bounded `48 -> 32 -> no-anchor fallback` sequence before accepting unnecessary evidence loss
   - Preserve the Serato clickable-card regression: a model `card` result with destination and target-matched OCR must remain reviewable through OCR-derived coordinates, while unrelated cards remain excluded and no autonomous click is approved
   - Compare anchored vs unanchored `recognition_plan_v1` traces on the same screenshot set
   - Track whether OCR anchors improve visual-only/icon-adjacent region labels, bbox placement, top-k ranking, and fallback frequency
   - Keep full-anchor runs only as controlled comparisons; production-style `click_target` prompts should retain complete OCR in traces but send the bounded `relation_matrix_compact` text-coordinate projection
   - Compare `prompt_focus_neighbor_limit` values for OCR-matched label-adjacent icons so local layout evidence improves without exceeding the prompt token budget
   - Inspect `anchor_relations` and `grounding_constraints` on returned visual regions to check whether the model used the intended OCR anchors as edge, center, size, and exclusion evidence before trusting the bbox
   - Track `grounding_constraints.grounding_evaluation`: visual-only icon boxes should exclude text anchors, while text-bearing controls should include referenced text anchors or expose an `anchor_corrected_bbox`
   - Use the latest compact-anchor MouseTester smoke as a baseline: `77/77` anchors reached the model without fallback, but the 8B model filled `grounding_constraints` more reliably than `anchor_relations`
   - Treat VISTA-4B Transformers as the active locate/execute grounding baseline, with Qwen3-VL understanding models feeding PathGraph/recall evidence before point grounding
   - Preserve the historical 2026-05-26 single-target precision, QQ close-button, and token-budget regressions as archived comparison cases, but do not use Qwen3.6 35B as the current local backend baseline
   - If a model returns `0..1000` coordinates, recover them before clamping and keep the restored candidate subject to review/confirmation
   - Dense OCR screens should still project to a bounded relation matrix that preserves selected text and avoids anchor fallback, regardless of which understanding model is active
   - Track the measured relation-output gap: the matrix prompt asks for relevant `anchor_relations`, but the QQ close-button smoke still returned an empty list; decide whether later execution policy should reject or deterministically supplement missing relation provenance
   - Keep the two-local-model split measurable: `local_understanding` handles whole-screen learning/observation while VISTA handles precise point localization before gated execution.
   - Treat `Qwen3-VL 8B Q4_K_M` as the launchable default for Learn Mode / `local_understanding`; keep `Qwen3-VL 4B Q4_K_M` as the optional fast understanding profile, and expand benchmark cases because the current 2-case smoke still misses small SEEK filter text such as Pay/posted metadata.
   - Keep historical InternVL3.5 and Qwen3.6 notes only as comparison evidence
   - Keep MiniCPM-V-4.6 as a benchmark-only option until the runtime has a compatible service path; do not wire it into normal model start/execute flow yet
   - If full-page anchors still drift, crop the target card first and rerun grounding in a local coordinate system
   - Keep the unanchored provider retry as the rollback path when anchored prompts fail

8. Later, start semantic generalization only after the stable loop is measurable.
   - Compare equivalent intent labels such as `寮€濮嬫祴閫焋, `Launch Test`, `Run Benchmark`, and `Start`
   - Add negative examples so semantic matching does not over-click unrelated controls

## Definition Of Done For The Next Phase

- The MouseTester single-page stable loop can be repeated across sessions.
- Successful `execute_recognition_plan` runs create a reusable learning record with trace, target, point, evidence, and validation metadata.
- Learned records can be replayed or consulted in later runs, but fresh observation, pre-click gating, and post-click validation remain mandatory.
- The report measures top-1 recognition, selected point drift, pre-click allow/reject, execution attempt count, semantic verification, READ icon candidates, UIA matches, and screen-reading rank contribution.
- Failures are attributed to a concrete layer rather than only reported as a failed click.
- The current live execution route remains gated by `pre_click_decision_v1` and continues to refuse weak evidence.
- Real Edge/MouseTester UIA quality remains represented in repeatable trace/evaluation evidence.
- The narrowest relevant tests pass, then the full suite passes.

## Do Not Do Yet

- Do not allow unrestricted autonomous browsing.
- Do not execute clicks from raw full-screen vision coordinates.
- Do not treat local OCR text that matches the candidate itself as enough evidence if the candidate does not match the user goal.
- Do not broaden to many websites before MouseTester execution and verification are measurable.
- Do not start unknown-page autonomy before the Single-page stable loop and cross-session stability are trace-backed.
- Do not let visual-only icon candidates execute from label or context guesses alone; require sufficient UIA/browser accessibility, shape/template evidence, or learned memory confirmation.
