## SEEK profile readiness gate (2026-06-17)

Before live safe-fill, run:

```powershell
uv run python scripts\seek_profile_readiness.py `
  --candidate-profile path\to\candidate_profile.json `
  --out logs\smoke\seek_profile_readiness.json `
  --fail-if-blocked
```

If a template is needed:

```powershell
uv run python scripts\seek_profile_readiness.py `
  --write-template artifacts\seek\candidate_profile_template.json `
  --out logs\smoke\seek_profile_readiness_template.json
```

Do not run `--fill-safe-fields` unless `candidate_profile_readiness_v1.decision` is `ready_for_single_safe_field_live_smoke` and `live_smoke_ready=true`. `safe_fill_ready=true` alone is not enough; the profile must also include matching basics (`skills` or `target_roles`, plus `location_constraints`), `experience_summary`, and `work_rights_summary`. The next safe runtime check remains real-profile no-apply traversal + matching first, then Apply Entry read-only only for `strong_apply`, then at most one low-risk text/email/url/tel field with post-fill verification.

When a real profile is available, include `avoid_roles`, `excluded_roles`, `avoid_companies`, `do_not_apply_to`, and `preferred_work_modes` if relevant. The scorer now uses these fields before saving suitable jobs. If a job mentions visa sponsorship, citizenship/residency, security clearance, police checks, or background checks, expect `need_user_review` rather than automatic Apply Entry.

After every SEEK run, inspect `seek_mvp_run_report_v1.accuracy_summary` first. It should show `final_submissions_zero=true`, `submit_clicks_zero=true`, `wrong_scope_scrolls_zero=true`, and `status=pass`. If `status=needs_review`, open the referenced card-click, scroll, detail, or Apply Entry trace before changing heuristics.

## SEEK card extraction accuracy next step (2026-06-17)

Before another live Apply/safe-fill push, finish the card-candidate accuracy pass:

- Synthetic result-list cards are now stricter: they require a card anchor such as location or company plus SEEK metadata, reject detail classification text, reject `Background` section headings as companies, and record `synthetic_validation` evidence.
- `screen_inventory_v1.cards` now dedupes high-overlap candidates with the same company/location, preferring UIA-backed, complete titles over mixed screen labels or incomplete hyperlink labels.
- Real no-apply follow-up smoke `logs\smoke\seek_mvp_readonly_after_title_merge_3jobs_20260617.json` reached `jobs_seen=3`, `jobs_opened=3`, `post_click_layout_drift_count=0`, `card_click_open_rate=1.0`, `wrong_scope_scroll_count=0`, `submit_clicks=0`, and `final_submissions=0`.
- Detail completeness no longer hard-requires a `responsibilities` heading. Default role evidence now accepts either `requirements` or `responsibilities`; both missing still triggers a `role_evidence` scroll request. Recomputing the same smoke with the new rule gives `fully_read_recomputed=3/3`.
- Fresh no-apply confirmation `logs\smoke\seek_mvp_readonly_after_role_evidence_3jobs_20260617.json` directly writes `jobs_seen=3`, `jobs_opened=3`, `jobs_fully_read=3`, `detail_read_completion_rate=1.0`, `card_click_open_rate=1.0`, `post_click_layout_drift_count=0`, `wrong_scope_scroll_count=0`, `submit_clicks=0`, `final_submissions=0`, and `accuracy_summary.status=pass`.
- 5-job follow-up fixed report alignment after a drift step and duplicate queue keys caused by OCR punctuation/stuck-title differences. The previous Vista Group synthetic-card drift was fixed by sending `seeded_candidate_v1` card evidence into execution, preferring the seed click point after VISTA validates the seed ROI, capping wide SEEK results-list layout, filtering over-wide/noisy synthetic cards, rejecting viewed/ago status text, and rejecting incomplete card labels like `Engineering Manager -`.
- Latest no-apply report `logs\smoke\seek_mvp_readonly_after_parser_title_filter_5jobs_20260617.json` has `jobs_seen=5`, `jobs_opened=5`, `jobs_fully_read=5`, `opened_rate=1.0`, `card_click_open_rate=1.0`, `post_click_layout_drift_count=0`, `wrong_scope_scroll_count=0`, `submit_clicks=0`, and `final_submissions=0`.
- After that, use a real `candidate_profile_v1` that passes readiness. Run no-apply traversal + matching first; only then run Apply Entry read-only for `strong_apply`, still with no safe-fill unless the profile and single-field preview gates pass.

- 日常学习模式主界面只突出路径图、interface map、区域、按钮截图和状态跳转，减少开发排查工具对用户理解的干扰。
- 原有诊断入口保留，方便继续追 trace、回归报告和学习样本质量。

下一步：

1. 如果这些诊断工具仍常用，迁移到独立 `Diagnostics` 页面或设置抽屉，不继续占用学习模式主流程。
2. 为学习模式增加更直接的“一键查看学习结果”摘要：当前界面类型、已学固定按钮、动态区、危险区、可进入的下一状态。
3. 将高级诊断结果和 trace 审计结果统一成可读报告，而不是要求用户理解原始 JSON 路径。

5. 继续保持 final submit/send/confirm/payment 强拦截，不为了速度放宽最终提交保护。

- 坐标诊断已把 `python_homepage_saved_template_screenshot` 的旧 blocker 细化为 `root_cause=coordinate_space_recovery_needed` / `implicit_normalized_1000_recovery_needed`。provider 现在保留 raw parsed JSON 与 runtime normalized JSON，并且 Learn Recognition actual parser 默认启用 opt-in normalized_1000 recovery；全局默认和 Execute Mode 不变。
- Recorded provider raw_text replay 已验证这条坐标恢复能接入后半段展示：`logs\benchmarks\learn_recognition_python_saved_recorded_provider_replay_coordinate_recovery_v1\learn_actual_parser_smoke_report.json` 生成 4 个 grounding validations 和 4 个 display-only draft actions，但 `actual_model_call.attempted=0`，所以仍不算 fresh model ability。
- SEEK job card `open_detail` 语义已通过多卡 dry-run 与 replay 验证：`logs\benchmarks\learn_locator_card_open_detail_multi_probe_20260705\numbered_region_calibration_report.json` 有 2 个 job card dry-run pre-click 通过且 `real_clicks=0`；`logs\benchmarks\learn_locator_card_open_detail_multi_replay_transition_v1_20260705\actual_parser_output_v1.json` 生成 2 个 display-only `open_detail` action，并带 `learn_open_detail_transition_hint_v1`。该 hint 只供 PathGraph candidate review 使用，不授权 Execute。
- `learn_open_detail_transition_hint_v1` 已接入 PathGraph candidate 生成链路：同一 replay artifact 生成的 `artifacts\learning-draft-review\actual_parser_output_v1_fefeffe256\pathgraph_candidate\runtime_path_graph_candidate.json` 包含 `seek_results -> model_detail_view` 两条 `open_detail` 候选 transition，且仍为 `candidate_only=true`、`execute_binding_enabled=false`。
- Learning Draft 面板已经显示该候选边：action meta 显示 `transition=open_detail->detail_view`，Draft PathGraph preview 派生 `model_detail_view` 只读节点，Page Detail 显示 `Candidate transitions` 卡片。派生 detail 节点不会继承 source regions/actions，避免把未重新 observe 的详情页误当成已有证据。

- Learning Mode demo scaffold 已补上：`scripts\build_learn_demo_scaffold.py` / `POST /panel/create_learning_demo_scaffold` 会一次性刷新 precise-understanding、page-detail、integration readiness 和 current evidence sidecar，并生成 `learn_mode_demo_scaffold.json`。当前 SEEK smoke 为 4 个 artifact、0 个 failure、page-detail 可展示，但仍有 6 个 pending calibration。

# 2026-07-11 After triad element audit

1. Keep the three-image review contract mandatory for every protected or new-interface regression.
2. Expand golden elements only with independently annotated fixtures; do not tune expected boxes from current output.
3. Add a genuinely different fourth interface before making any broader reliability statement.
4. Keep model grounding separate: the current replays record zero model-grounding attempts and cannot support a model-accuracy claim.

# 2026-07-16 分层区域实验后的下一步

1. 暂不把 `hierarchical_region_partition_mvp_v1` 接入生产 Stage1，也不替换 `bar_detection_v1`。
2. MVP-2 已证明 coarse proposal 能补回 element-only 缺失区域；不要继续针对这三张图调 proposal 几何，也不要把第一轮失败扩大解释成混合方向不可行。
3. 如果继续，只做一个受限 adjudication-contract 实验：使用不能输出空 root parent、重复 proposal ownership 或非法 parent-child 的结构化输出协议；仍保持 one-shot、无 repair，并冻结 WhatsApp、Notepad、Python.org 输入。
4. 只有 coarse B 的 validator pass 数量高于 element-only A，且 Notepad 编辑区成为显式区域、WhatsApp 不再 over-merge/duplicate 后，才讨论加入新 holdout；在此之前不扩 Apple Music、Steam，也不进入生产流程。
5. 继续保持默认关闭、no Execute、no live click/fill/submit、no Runtime PathGraph promotion。

# 2026-07-16 MVP-2.1 后的下一步

1. 不再继续调整 selection-only prompt，也不再改这三个样本的 coarse proposal 几何；MVP-2.1 已隔离出剩余问题是模型 topology adjudication，而不是 parent/schema 生成。
2. 如果继续，只实现独立默认关闭的有限合法拓扑候选：程序从固定 proposals 生成 H1/H2/H3，8B 只选择最佳拓扑；不要再让模型自由生成区域树。
3. 下一实验仍先冻结 WhatsApp、Notepad、Python.org。只有 WhatsApp 能保留图标轨/会话列表/聊天区分离，同时 Notepad 编辑区和 Python.org 最低结构不退化，才加入独立 holdout。
4. 保持机器 topology validator 与三图人工审核分开；机器 3/3 不得宣传为模型可靠、accuracy 或通用识别成功率。
5. 继续保持默认关闭，不修改正式 Stage1、`bar_detection_v1`、Execute、PathGraph、VISTA 或 gate。

# 2026-07-16 MVP-2.2 后的下一步

1. 不再调整 selector prompt，也不扩大样本；先补一个真正打开聊天且 coarse proposals 中存在 composer child 的固定 WhatsApp fixture。
2. 继续使用同一 finite-hypothesis generator、validator 和冻结 reference 复跑，不能根据模型输出修改参考答案。
3. 只有 strict hypothesis recall 达到 3/3 后，才讨论 selector 是否值得增加独立样本；当前三个 `confidence=0.0` 输出不能作为可靠性证据。
4. 不复制或提交 ignored screenshots、models、runtime logs 和其他敏感资产；可复用人工 reference 只放在已审查的 `tests/fixtures/`。
5. 保持默认关闭，不修改正式 Stage1、`bar_detection_v1`、Execute、PathGraph、VISTA 或 gate。
## 2026-07-19 Post-review checkpoint

1. Keep Python.org, Apple Music, and Windows Settings as explicit semantic-review safe stops until independent evidence supports their requested roles; do not widen the role taxonomy merely to improve counts.
2. Add more frozen, checksum-pinned holdouts before discussing review-layer reliability. Keep `used_for_tuning=false` runs separate from development cases.
3. Improve overlay label layout as a presentation task without changing bbox geometry, group ownership, or Gate decisions.
4. Preserve final Stage2 path + numbering revision binding for every calibration request. A stale or pre-review report must remain blocked.
5. Keep all demonstrations display-only unless the user separately approves a reversible real action. Final submit, send, confirm, payment, live safe fill, and Runtime PathGraph promotion remain outside this checkpoint.
## 2026-07-20 Follow-up after full panel-flow verification

1. Keep the new resource attribution contract covered: launcher descendants and profile-reserved VRAM may explain known-model load, but measured external GPU processes must still constrain or block inference.
2. Clarify the calibration progress denominator in the panel. The displayed total currently includes review-only inventory while attempted/remaining counts describe calibration-eligible targets; do not present those numbers as one common denominator.
3. Add independent real read-only holdouts before making any claim about recognition or point-grounding reliability. Preserve capture checksum, final numbering revision, final overlay, page details, and read-only PathGraph identity for each run.
4. Preserve the no-action boundary: no live click, fill, submit, Execute binding, or Runtime PathGraph promotion is authorized by this checkpoint.

## 2026-07-20 Follow-up after QQ review repair

1. Add a common lower-toolbar/composer candidate-recall invariant using visual-control evidence, then replay QQ plus the protected non-chat interfaces before changing Stage1.5 boundaries.
2. Keep missing-region proposals conservative. Do not recover recall by accepting single OCR lines or by recreating semantic containers already represented by a parent pane.
3. Improve overlay evidence layering separately from recognition geometry so preserved atomic evidence remains auditable without obscuring the final semantic regions.
4. Add an independent chat holdout before discussing review-path reliability. The current fixed QQ replay proves that the protocol and integrity closure can complete, not that chat recognition is generally accurate.

## 2026-07-20 Follow-up after calibration-order and performance repair

1. Run one real read-only panel flow when GPU preflight is `normal` to measure per-batch wall time and confirm the batch can rise above `2`; repeat under constrained load only as a separate resource-behavior check.
2. Manually compare original/root/final images for the nine protected interfaces. Automated 9/9 root topology coverage is not a recognition-accuracy claim.
3. Keep model review after precise calibration and require it to consume the calibrated overlay plus the same Stage2 source revision.
4. Do not loosen Gate, enable clicks, or promote the read-only PathGraph while measuring calibration throughput.

## 2026-07-27 Branching workflow review continuation

1. Replace manually created placeholder transitions with consecutive learned-interface observations that carry a stable source node, operation identity, target node, capture lineage, and a verified destination state. Declarative destination text must remain visibly unverified until that observation exists.
2. Connect the Agent's unknown-interface decision to an explicit Learning Mode invocation and human-review pause. Loading application-scoped planning context is implemented; autonomous learn/review/publish/continue is not.

4. Run one complete user acceptance chain: capture unknown interface, learn, manually correct geometry and semantics, save, reload, publish reviewed memory, recapture current interface, dry-run grounding and Gate, then verify one reversible operation. Keep final submit, send, confirm, payment, and destructive actions blocked.
5. Add larger fixed workflow fixtures to tune graph layout for dense fan-out, convergence, and cycles without hiding nodes or reducing text below reviewable size.

1. Preserve the server-owned source resolution boundary for `/panel/compile_reviewed_workflow_asset` and `/panel/publish_reviewed_workflow_asset`; never accept a client-supplied source path or compiled asset as authority.
2. Preserve publish recompile, in-process workflow locking, final content-SHA guard, and CAS registry revision conflict handling. A blocked compile or conflict must leave the CAS unchanged.
3. Keep `/panel/preview_reviewed_workflow_replay` read-only and non-authorizing: require an explicit current observation, return `would_call_action_api=false` and `execution_authorized=false`, and do not hide capture/action calls behind preview.
4. Completed: the existing workflow-library panel owns a separate v2 state for Compile → CAS Publish → read-only Preview and invalidates stale state on workflow selection, load failure, edit, delete, or source revision change.
5. Completed offline: synthetic SEEK compile → publish → preview → two verified navigation envelopes reaches the apply-entry stop boundary with fake external dependencies and no GUI/network/action claim.
6. Track cross-process atomicity for the legacy v1 save/publish path as a later hardening item; it is not implied by the v2 in-process lock.
7. Completed offline: run the externally checksum-pinned recorded-Bare-event vs Runtime contract benchmark and retain classification, stop quality, bounded recovery, timing, and derived evidence digests. Do not describe it as live Agent or model accuracy.
8. Next: implement the production current-observation capture and live replay orchestrator that binds selection, grounding, Gate, the one-attempt action request, post-state verification, and recovery. Keep `read`/`scroll`/fill/upload/Continue/final-submit fail closed until their dedicated contracts exist; only then run a controlled local smoke.

## Universal Evidence Interface v1 M2 Shadow follow-up

1. Preserve the Disabled M1 static-fixture sidecar and the M2 Shadow runtime as separate non-authorizing boundaries; neither immutable evidence nor a provider result grants action permission.
2. Wait for fixed device-0 free GPU memory to reach the required 8 GiB, then run the approved local/offline smoke. Its closed benchmark path performs exactly one cold plus three warm inferences inside one worker/model lifetime, uses a distinct provider-version identity, and persists the sealed success store/report under ignored local artifacts. Installed local runtime and weights are verified; do not download weights, use network, or claim the smoke before the resource preflight passes.
3. Keep the generic runtime limited to registered enabled `local_only` Shadow adapters, fixed server-owned immutable refs/store, fixed trusted worker configuration, bounded process/resource cleanup, and sealed result/receipt evidence.
4. Keep Learning Draft and panel output compact display-only/review-only. Do not expose items/text/bboxes/source IDs/transforms/full refs or create provider-run, action, grounding, replay, PathGraph, memory, capture, GUI, network, or Execute integration.
5. Before any broader integration, require a separately approved milestone with explicit current-capture binding, provider lifecycle, action/Gate boundaries, privacy review, and remote-egress policy.
## 2026-08-24 Five-Screen Provider Decision

- 冻结决定：OmniParser 替换 Qwen 为 `NO-GO`；不要修改 Qwen semantic defaults，也不要把 OmniParser 设为默认 candidate provider。
- 主要质量缺口（修正后的有效五屏重跑）：Omni micro F1 `0.256579`、precision `0.193069`、recall `0.382353`；40 个关键目标 `0` 个直接 accept，canonical role coverage `0`。
- 下一步若继续 OmniParser，只做 provider adapter 后处理实验：候选去重/置信度过滤、OCR/图标 caption 规范化、UIA 交叉证据融合，并在**同一冻结五屏**重新跑门；不得降低门槛或用单屏结果替代五屏结果。
- Qwen 当前也不是高质量控件检测器；它继续承担 semantic inventory baseline，而不是被描述为精确 bbox provider。VISTA 保持 goal-conditioned locator，不能拿 `17/40` 的本次结果证明通用定位能力。
- 稀疏无 OCR 的官方 OmniParser 崩溃已在公共 wrapper 修复并有 regression；后续要保留 Snipping Tool 作为稀疏界面 negative control。
